<h3><a href="https://www.threads.com/@saars.foto/post/Dewkudim4IN">Water Wednesday 💧 Rivers, rain on glass, ocean edges, puddles, fountains - show me water doing its thing. Framed this one through the railing at Niagara - the falls did the rest ONE photo. Replying is consent: 3 favorites featured tomorrow and pinned to the profile, and may also be featured in an Instagram reel or weekend IG roundup with full credit. Best of the week lands on Instagram on the weekend.</a></h3>
